Let's detail `linear_conv` and `score`:
```
def _next_pow2(x):
    return 1 << (max(0, int(np.ceil(np.log2(x)))))
```

Example:
```
def make_ctx(n):
   lin_size = next_pow2(2*n-1)
   cross_size = next_pow2(3*n-2)
   idx = np.arange(2*n-2, n-1, -1) # length n? from L-1 down to n-1

Let's design `mirror_phase` in detail:
```
def mirror_phase(a, n, gamma, max_iters, deadline, ctx, rng, best_score, best_seq):
    lin_size,cross_size,ctx_idx=ctx
    a=normalize(a)
    best_score_local=best_score
    best_seq_local=best_seq
    parent=a
    # compute initial parent info
    score_p, G_p, b = get_conv_sum1(parent,gamma,ctx)
    if not finite: return parent,...
    no=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       if score_p >= best_score_local: # no need? still can find better via G
       # recompute weights from b parent (already have b)
       if b is None or not finite: break
       mb=b.max(); if mb<=0: break
       theta=gamma/mb
       # weights normalized
       t=theta*(b-mb)
       w=np.exp(t) # max1
       ws=w.sum()
       if not finite or ws==0: w=b/np.sum(b)
       else: w/=ws
       c=cross_correlate(parent,w,ctx)
       s=2*c
       mean=s.mean()
       d=s-mean
       std=d.std()
       if std <= 1e-15:
           no +=10
       else:
           dd=d/std
           # Try etas adaptively
           accepted=False
           # Maybe scale dd by min(1, ?) no.
           for eta in (0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.003):
              arg=np.clip(-eta*dd, -10, 10)
              cand=normalize(parent*np.exp(arg))
              score_c,G_c,_=get_conv_sum1(cand,gamma,ctx)
              if not finite: continue
              accept=False
              if score_c + 1e-12 < parent_score:
                  accept=True
              elif G_c + 1e-12 < G_p and (score_c <= parent_score + 1e-7*parent_score or no>5):
                  accept=True
              elif no>20 and score_c < parent_score + 0.01*parent_score and G_c < G_p:
                  accept=True
              if accept:
                  parent=cand; parent_score=score_c; G_p=G_c
                  accepted=True
                  no=0
                  if parent_score < best_score_local:
                      best_score_local=parent_score
                      best_seq_local=parent.copy()
                  break
           if not accepted:
              no +=1
              # occasional random nudge
              if no > 15:
                 sigma=0.05 if no<30 else 0.1
                 cand=normalize(parent*np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n)))
                 score_c,G_c,_=get_conv_sum1(cand,gamma,ctx)
                 if score_c < parent_score or (G_c < G_p - 1e-9 and score_c < parent_score + 0.02*parent_score):
                     parent=cand; parent_score=score_c; G_p=G_c; no=0
                     if score_c < best_score_local: update
                 else:
                     no +=5
              if no > 60: break
       # update b for next iteration? We need b parent. If accepted recompute; if not, maybe still from before? We skipped recompute. At top of loop recompute to ensure current.
       # To avoid double, recompute at start each iteration.
       if time budget...:
          score_p,G_p,b=get_conv_sum1(parent,gamma,ctx)
```
This recomputes b twice? In loop after acceptance, we don't recompute immediately until next top. Fine.
But line search uses parent_score and G_p. Initially set. At loop start, recompute if changed? We can structure:
```
while time<deadline:
   score_p,G_p,b = get_conv_sum1(parent,...)
   if score_p < best: update
   ... weights from b
   ... line search; if accepted update parent/score
   if not accepted: maybe break
```

`peak_hill_climb` can be called after mirror phases for exact max. It can use similar but linear direction. Implementation:
```
def peak_hill_climb(a, n, max_iters, deadline, ctx, best_score, best_seq):
    parent=normalize(a)
    best_score_local=best_score; best_seq_local=best_seq
    no=0
    while time<deadline:
       b=linear_conv(parent,ctx); mb=b.max(); score=2*n*mb
       if score < best: update
       # top peaks
       topk=min(16,n)
       peaks=np.argpartition(-b, topk)[:topk]
       s=np.zeros(n)
       for k in peaks:
          k=int(k); start=max(0,k-n+1); stop=min(n,k+1)
          j_end=k-(stop-1); j_start=k-start
          s[start:stop] += 2*parent[j_end:j_start+1][::-1]
       s /= len(peaks)
       d=s-s.mean(); std=d.std()
       if std<1e-15: no+=1; continue
       scale=np.median(parent)/(std+1e-15)
       accepted=False
       for f in (0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.5,0.01):
          cand=parent + (-f*scale*d)  # direction to reduce max
          cand=np.clip(cand,0,None); cand=normalize(cand)
          cscore=score_sum(cand,ctx)
          if cscore < score - 1e-12:
             parent=cand; score=cscore; update; accepted=True; no=0; break
       if not accepted:
          no+=1
          if no>30: break
          # random block
```
This might be slow due evaluate many candidates. Use few etas.
But mirror phase already does multiplicative which may be better. Peak linear can clip.

Could combine `exact_polish` with random block improvements:
- After mirror, do while time: if no polynomial? Use random block multiplicative with sigma schedule, accept hill climb improvements. Since base not constant, hill climb works. This is like previous program but starting from inverse sqrt. Implement simple `hill_climb_random(a, sigma, max_steps, deadline)`:
   for many steps: candidate=block multiply or full exp random; eval; if improves update. If stuck no_improve, increase sigma or break.
This complement.

```
def try_sequence(a, n, time_limit, deadline, ctx, rng, best_score, best_seq):
    if time.monotonic()>=deadline: return
    ctx=make_context(n)
    # gamma schedule with fractions of time_limit
    fractions = [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.25,0.2,0.1]
    gammas=[1,3,10,30,80,200]
    for f,g in zip(fr,gammas):
        phase_deadline = time + f*time_limit
        a,best_score,best_seq = mirror_phase(a,n,g,phase_deadline,ctx,rng,best...)
        if no best improvement and g>30? maybe break
    # polish exact for remaining small
    a,best_score,best_seq = peak_hill_climb(a,n, min(10 time), ... )
    a,best_score,best_seq = random_hill(a,n, remaining, ...)
```

Let's implement next_power_of_two without log to avoid overflow:
```
def next_power_of_two(x):
    if x <=1: return 1
    return 1 << int(np.ceil(np.log2(x)))
```
For x Python int. Use bit_length:
```
def next_power_of_two(x):
    return 1 << (max(0, int(np.ceil(np.log2(x)))))
```
Or `x = int(x); return 1 << (x-1).bit_length()` for >=1. Use bit_length for speed:
```
if x <=1: return 1
return 1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length()
```

   theta=gamma/mb

   tmax=t.max()
   # sum exp(t-tmax)
   # if tmax too large? theta*b max=gamma <= maybe 500; theta*mb=gamma; okay.

else: G=score

Normalize:
```
def normalize(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
       a=np.ones(a.shape,dtype=np.float64)
       s=a.size
   a = a / s
   # Avoid zeros? maybe if any zero set small?
   return a
```
If we want positive for mirror, zeros can be increased by exp; if a_i zero, cand zero*exp= zero, stays zero. To allow recovery, add tiny floor before mirror: `a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300); a/=a.sum()`. This adds tiny mass. Do in normalize? It may distort but 1e-300 negligible. Use `floor=1e-300` always. But for n=16384, floor* n ~1e-295, negligible. Good.

def random_hill_climb(a,n,deadline,ctx,best_score,best_seq,rng,no_improve_initial=0):
   parent=normalize(a); best...
   score=score_sum(parent,ctx)
   no=0
   sigma=0.05
   while time<deadline:
      for attempt in range(20):
         style = rng.integers(0,2)
         if style==0: sigma= min(0.5, 0.02*(1+no/10))
         cand=random...
         cscore=score_sum(cand,ctx)
         if cscore < best: update
         if cscore < score - 1e-12:
            parent=cand; score=cscore; no=0; break
      else:
         no +=1
         if no%10==0: sigma*=1.2
         if no>100: break
   # final polish maybe small sigma 0.01 hill climb
```

def run_mirror_schedule(a,n,start_time,total_time,deadline,rng,best...):

   fracs=[...]

Implement exact fallback:
```
def exact_score_numpy(seq):
   a=np.asarray(seq,dtype=float)
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   s=a.sum()
   if s <0.01: return np.inf
   b=np.convolve(a,a)
   mb=b.max()
   return float(2*a.size*mb/(s*s))
```
For final exact, first try evaluate_sequence on list; if exception, exact_score_numpy.

`normalize_sequence`:
```
def normalize_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim !=1:
       a=a.reshape(-1)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    # If all zero, return ones
    s = float(a.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(a.shape, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size)
    # floor
    a = a / s
    # Add tiny floor if any zero? Could change; do:
    if a.size > 0:
       a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
       s = a.sum();
       if s>0: a=a/s
    return a
```
Floor after normalize: sum slightly >1, renormalize. Good.

`linear_convolution`:
```
def linear_convolution(a, ctx):
    lin_size = ctx[0]
    n = a.size
    if n == 0: return np.array([])
    fa = np.fft.fft(a, n=lin_size)
    b = np.fft.ifft(fa*fa, n=lin_size).real
    L=2*n-1
    b = b[:L]
    # Clamp small negative
    # Due to FFT maybe tiny negative; use np.maximum
    return np.maximum(b, 0.0)
```
Clamping all negative to 0 may slightly alter sum; okay. For score and weights, positive.

`get_conv_sum1`:
```
def get_conv_sum1(a, gamma, ctx):
    n=a.size
    b=linear_convolution(a,ctx)
    if b.size==0: return np.inf, np.inf, b
    mb=float(b.max())
    score=2.0*n*mb
    if mb <= 0.0 or gamma <= 0.0:
       return score, score, b
    theta=gamma/mb
    t=theta*b
    tmax=float(t.max())
    if not np.isfinite(tmax): return score, np.inf,b
    # If tmax large, exp(t-tmax)
    # To avoid sum under/overflow, clip lower? exp negative underflow okay.
    e=np.exp(t - tmax)
    se=float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
       G=score
    else:
       G=(tmax + float(np.log(se))) / theta
    return score, G, b
```
Since sum a=1, b sum should 1. If not due floor, small; score formula uses max only but sum maybe not exactly 1. Wait if we floor after normalize, sum exactly 1 (renormalized). Good. Score=2n*mb. If a sum1.

`cross_correlate`:
```
def cross_correlate(a, weights, ctx):
    n=a.size; cross_size=ctx[1]; idx=ctx[2]
    # weights length 2n-1, maybe ensure
    if weights.size != 2*n-1:
       # trim/pad
       w=np.zeros(2*n-1); m=min(weights.size,2*n-1); w[:m]=weights[:m]; weights=w
    # sparse if few positive
    # Use threshold relative to max
    wmax=float(weights.max()) if weights.size else 0
    if wmax > 0:
       thr = max(1e-14, 1e-12*wmax)
       idxs=np.nonzero(weights > thr)[0]
    else: idxs=empty
    if idxs.size < 256:
       c=np.zeros(n)
       for kk in idxs:
          k=int(kk)
          start=k-(n-1)
          if start < 0: start=0
          stop=k+1
          if stop > n: stop=n
          if stop <= start: continue
          j_end=k-(stop-1)
          j_start=k-start
          # ensure 0<=j_end<=j_start<n
          if j_end <0: j_end=0
          if j_start >= n: j_start=n-1
          val=float(weights[kk])
          c[start:stop] += val * a[j_end:j_start+1][::-1]
       return c
    else:
       w_rev=weights[::-1]
       fa=np.fft.fft(a, n=cross_size)
       fw=np.fft.fft(w_rev, n=cross_size)
       full=np.fft.ifft(fa*fw).real
       return full[idx]
```
Need if idxs.size==0 returns zeros. For dense, full real may have tiny imaginary; okay. `idx` length n from start to stop; ensure full length >= idx max+1. cross_size>=3n-2, full length cross_size. idx max=2n-2 < cross_size. Good.
In sparse, `j_start` can be < j_end? Shouldn't. Example k large: start=k-n+1, j_end=n-1? Wait for k=2n-2, start=n-1, stop=n, j_end=n-1,j_start=n-1. Good.

`mirror_phase` more detailed:
```
def mirror_phase(a, gamma, phase_deadline, ctx, rng, best_score, best_seq):
    n=a.size
    parent=normalize_sequence(a)
    best_score_local=best_score
    best_seq_local=best_seq
    # compute initial
    no=0
    last_best_time=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic() < phase_deadline:
        score_p, G_p, b = get_conv_sum1(parent, gamma, ctx)
        if not np.isfinite(score_p): break
        if score_p < best_score_local:
            best_score_local=score_p; best_seq_local=parent.copy(); last_best_time=time.monotonic()
        if b.size == 0: break
        mb=float(b.max())
        if mb <= 0: break
        theta=gamma/mb
        t=theta*(b-mb)
        # t max 0
        # weights normalized
        e=np.exp(t)
        ws=float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
            ws_b=float(b.sum())
            if ws_b >0: w=b/ws_b
            else: break
        else:
            w=e/ws
        c=cross_correlate(parent, w, ctx)
        s=2.0*c
        mean_s=float(s.mean())
        d=s-mean_s
        std=float(d.std())
        if not np.isfinite(std) or std <= 1e-18:
            no += 5
        else:
            dd=d/std
            accepted=False
            # Etas: try robust order
            for eta in (0.2, 0.08, 0.03, 0.012, 0.005):
                arg=-eta*dd
                # Clip to avoid extreme
                arg=np.clip(arg, -10.0, 10.0)
                cand=normalize_sequence(parent * np.exp(arg))
                score_c, G_c, _ = get_conv_sum1(cand, gamma, ctx)
                if not np.isfinite(score_c): continue
                accept=False
                if score_c + 1e-12 < score_p:
                    accept=True
                else:
                    # allow smoothed descent if max not worse by too much
                    if G_c + 1e-12 < G_p:
                        tol = 1e-7 + 1e-4*(no/10.0)
                        if score_c <= score_p * (1.0 + tol):
                            accept=True
                if accept:
                    parent=cand
                    score_p=score_c
                    G_p=G_c
                    accepted=True
                    no=0
                    if score_p < best_score_local:
                        best_score_local=score_p; best_seq_local=parent.copy()
                    break
            if not accepted:
                no +=1
                if no % 10 == 0:
                    # small random smooth nudge
                    sigma = 0.05 if no < 40 else 0.1
                    noise = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n)
                    cand=normalize_sequence(parent * np.exp(np.clip(noise, -6, 6)))
                    score_c, G_c, _ = get_conv_sum1(cand, gamma, ctx)
                    if np.isfinite(score_c):
                        if score_c < score_p or (G_c < G_p and score_c < score_p*1.01):
                            parent=cand; score_p=score_c; G_p=G_c; no=0
                            if score_p < best_score_local: update
                if no > 100:
                    break
    # After phase, keep best parent? Return parent and best
    return parent, best_score_local, best_seq_local
```
At no>100 break. The random nudge may accept worse score if G improves and within 1%. Good.
Potential issue: candidate G uses gamma and its mb. If score_p is much larger than G_p? G≤score. If candidate score improves but G maybe worsens, accept. Good.
If gamma high, G close score; okay.

`peak_hill_climb` details:
```
def peak_hill_climb(a, phase_deadline, ctx, rng, best_score, best_seq):
    n=a.size
    parent=normalize_sequence(a)
    best_score_local=best_score; best_seq_local=best_seq
    no=0
    while time.monotonic() < phase_deadline:
       b=linear_convolution(parent, ctx)
       mb=b.max(); score=2*n*mb
       if score < best_score_local: update
       topk=32 if b.size>32 else b.size
       peaks=np.argpartition(-b, topk-1)[:topk] if topk>0 else np.array([])
       s=np.zeros(n)
       for kk in peaks:
          k=int(kk)
          start=max(0,k-n+1); stop=min(n,k+1)
          if stop<=start: continue
          j_end=k-(stop-1); j_start=k-start
          s[start:stop] += 2.0 * parent[j_end:j_start+1][::-1]
       if s.std() > 1e-18:
          s /= s.sum()? no, use gradient average? For direction, scaling by factor; we can use s as is.
          d=s - s.mean()
          std=d.std()
          scale=float(np.median(parent))/std
          accepted=False
          for f in (0.5,0.2,0.08,0.03,0.012):
             cand=parent + (-f*scale*d)
             cand=np.clip(cand,0,None); cand=normalize_sequence(cand)
             cscore=exact? use score_sum
             csc,_,_=get_conv_sum1(cand,0,ctx)
             if csc + 1e-12 < score:
                parent=cand; score=cscore; accepted=True; no=0; if score<best update; break
          if not accepted:
             no+=1
             # add random block?
       else: break
       if no>50: break
    return parent,best_score_local,best_seq_local
```
But linear direction `parent - f*scale*d`: d scaled by parent median/std. If d has negative values, adding can go positive. Good.
Potential: If direction is wrong (score increases), try signs? The subgradient for reducing max should be -d. But if multiple peaks, okay. We can try both - and + for the smallest factor? Maybe not.

Since mirror phase may already do equivalent multiplicative, peak might not add much. We can include small time.

def random_hill_climb(a, phase_deadline, ctx, rng, best_score, best_seq):
    n=a.size
    parent=normalize_sequence(a)
    best_score_local=best_score; best_seq_local=best_seq
    score=score_sum? use get_conv_sum1 parent gamma0
    no=0
    sigma_block=0.2
    while time < deadline:
       for attempt in range(30):
           if rng.random() < 0.5:
              cand=parent.copy()
              width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//200), max(2,n//8)))
              start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
              factor=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma_block)))
              cand[start:start+width]*=factor
              # optionally local shift? no
           else:
              noise=rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=n)
              cand=parent*np.exp(np.clip(noise,-4,4))
           cand=normalize_sequence(cand)
           csc,_,_=get_conv_sum1(cand,0,ctx)
           if not finite continue
           if csc < best: update
           if csc < score - 1e-12:
               parent=cand; score=csce; no=0; break
       else:
          no +=1
          if no%5==0: sigma_block = min(1.0, sigma_block*1.2)
          if no>200: break
    # final small sigma hill climb? Could do one more loop with sigma_block=0.05 for remaining if no? maybe.
    return parent,best_score_local,best_seq_local
```

`make_base_sequence`:
```
def make_base_sequence(n, variant, rng=None):
   idx=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
   t=idx/(max(1,n-1))
   if variant == 'inv05':
      a=(idx+0.5)**-0.5
   elif 'inv1':
      a=(idx+1.0)**-0.5
   elif 'inv_taper':
      a=(idx+0.5)**-0.5 * (1.0 + 0.5*t)
   elif 'inv_sin':
      a=(idx+0.5)**-0.5 * (1.0 + 0.3*np.sin(np.pi*t))
   elif 'constant': a=np.ones(n)
   elif 'random':
      if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
      a=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.5,n))
   else: inv05
   return normalize_sequence(a)
```
Maybe include variant 'inverse_soft' = 1/sqrt(i+0.5) * (1+ alpha log?)
Not necessary.

```
def optimize_start(a,n,start_time,time_budget,deadline,rng,best_score,best_seq):
   ctx=make_context(n)
   # schedule fractions relative to time_budget
   schedule = [(1.0,0.05),(2.5,0.10),(6.0,0.20),(15.0,0.25),(35.0,0.18),(80.0,0.12),(200.0,0.10)]
   cum=0.0
   for gamma, frac in schedule:
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       phase_deadline = min(deadline, start_time + (cum+frac)*time_budget)
       if phase_deadline <= time.monotonic(): phase_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic()+max(2.0, 0.5*time_budget))
       a, best_score, best_seq = mirror_phase(a,gamma,phase_deadline,ctx,rng,best_score,best_seq)
       cum += frac
       # occasional exact polish? maybe after gamma 15
   # remaining exact/random if before deadline
   if time.monotonic() < deadline:
       rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
       # peak hill 10% rem, random 30%
       t_peak = time.monotonic() + 0.1*rem
       t_rand = min(deadline, t_peak + 0.5*rem)
       a,best_score,best_seq=peak_hill_climb(a,t_peak,ctx,rng,best_score,best_seq)
       a,best_score,best_seq=random_hill_climb(a,t_rand,ctx,rng,best_score,best_seq)
   return a,best_score,best_seq,ctx
```

```
def exact_score_numpy(seq):
    a=np.asarray(seq,dtype=float)
    if a.ndim!=1: a=a.reshape(-1)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    a=np.minimum(a,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s < 0.01: return np.inf
    b=np.convolve(a,a)
    mb=b.max()
    return float(2*a.size*mb/(s*s))
```
Matches evaluator (except evaluator clips after conversion, same). Good.

Let's write quick search:
```
def quick_param_search(n, ctx, deadline, rng, best_score, best_seq):
   idx=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
   if n>1: t=idx/(n-1)
   else: t=np.zeros(1)
   powers=(0.25,0.35,0.45,0.5,0.55,0.65,0.8)
   offs=(0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.5)
   mods=[0,1,2,3,4]
   best_local=best_score; best_local_seq=best_seq
   for p in powers:
     for off in offs:
       base=(idx+off)**(-p)
       mods_arrays = [base, base*(1.0+0.4*t), base*(1.0-0.3*t), base*(1.0+0.3*np.sin(np.pi*t)), base*(1.0+0.3*np.cos(np.pi*t))]
       for arr in mods_arrays:
          if time.monotonic()>deadline: return ...
          a=normalize_sequence(arr)
          sc,_,_=get_conv_sum1(a,0,ctx)
          if sc<best_local: update
   return best_local,best_local_seq
```

Let's implement schedule in `optimize_start`:
```
def optimize_start(a, n, ctx, start_time, time_budget, deadline, rng, best_score, best_seq):
    # quick param? maybe do outside.
    parent=normalize_sequence(a)
    schedule=[(2.0,0.08),(5.0,0.12),(10.0,0.22),(20.0,0.22),(40.0,0.16),(80.0,0.12),(160.0,0.08)]
    cum=0.0
    total = max(1.0, min(time_budget, max(0.0, deadline-start_time)))
    for gamma, frac in schedule:
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       end = start_time + (cum + frac)*total
       if end <= time.monotonic():
           end = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + max(5.0, 0.02*total))
       parent, best_score, best_seq = mirror_phase(parent, gamma, end, ctx, rng, best_score, best_seq)
       cum += frac
       # if best score very low? still continue? maybe.
    # polish
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
       parent = normalize_sequence(best_seq)
       rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
       t_peak = time.monotonic() + 0.15*rem
       t_peak = min(t_peak, deadline)
       parent, best_score, best_seq = peak_hill_climb(parent, t_peak, ctx, rng, best_score, best_seq)
       t_rand = time.monotonic() + 0.5*rem
       t_rand = min(t_rand, deadline)
       parent, best_score, best_seq = random_hill_climb(parent, t_rand, ctx, rng, best_score, best_seq)
    return parent, best_score, best_seq
```

```
def sort_pool(pool):
   # simple insertion for len <=5
   for i in range(1,len(pool)):
      item=pool[i]; j=i-1
      while j>=0 and pool[j][0] > item[0]: pool[j+1]=pool[j]; j-=1
      pool[j+1]=item

Detailed `quick_param_search`:
```
def quick_param_search(n, ctx, deadline, rng, best_score, best_seq):
    idx=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
    if n>1: t=idx/(n-1)
    else: t=np.zeros(1,dtype=np.float64)
    best_seq_local=normalize_sequence(best_seq)
    best_score_local=float(best_score)
    powers=(0.30,0.42,0.50,0.60)
    offs=(0.25,0.50,0.75,1.0)
    for p in powers:
      for off in offs:
        base=(idx+off)**(-p)
        # arrays
        arrs=[]
        arrs.append(base)
        arrs.append(base*(1.0+0.35*t))
        arrs.append(base*(1.0-0.25*t))
        arrs.append(base*(1.0+0.3*np.sin(np.pi*t)))
        arrs.append(base + 0.4*base[::-1])
        for arr in arrs:
           if time.monotonic() > deadline: return best_seq_local,best_score_local
           a=normalize_sequence(arr)
           sc,_,_=get_conv_sum1(a,0.0,ctx)
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score_local:
               best_score_local=sc; best_seq_local=a.copy()
    return best_seq_local,best_score_local
```
If arr contains negative due 1-0.25t positive, no. base+ reverse positive.

def make_base_sequence(n, variant, rng=None):
   idx=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
   t=idx/(n-1) if n>1 else zeros
   if variant=='constant': arr=np.ones(n)
   elif variant=='inv05': arr=(idx+0.5)**-0.5
   elif variant=='inv1': arr=(idx+1.0)**-0.5
   elif variant=='inv_ramp': arr=(idx+0.5)**-0.5 * (1.0 + 0.5*t)
   elif variant=='inv_revramp': arr=(idx+0.5)**-0.5 * (1.0 + 0.5*(1-t))
   elif variant=='sin': arr=np.ones + 0.3 sin(pi t)
   elif variant=='random':
       if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
       arr=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.5,n))
   elif variant=='height': ...
   else inv05
   return normalize_sequence(arr)
```

       if hs.size>0 and hs.sum()>0:
          x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,hs.size)
          x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
          h=np.interp(x_new,x_old,hs)
          h=normalize_sequence(h)
          base=normalize_sequence((idx+0.5)**-0.5)
          arr=0.4*h+0.6*base

       pass

`optimize_start`:
```
def optimize_start(a,n,ctx,start_time,time_budget,deadline,rng,best_score,best_seq):
    if time_budget <=0: return normalize_sequence(a), best_score, best_seq
    parent=normalize_sequence(a)
    total=min(float(time_budget), max(0.0, deadline-start_time))
    schedule=[(2.0,0.08),(5.0,0.12),(10.0,0.22),(20.0,0.22),(40.0,0.16),(80.0,0.12),(160.0,0.08)]
    cum=0.0
    for gamma,frac in schedule:
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       end=start_time + (cum+frac)*total
       if end <= time.monotonic():
           end=min(deadline, time.monotonic()+max(2.0, 0.01*total))
       parent,best_score,best_seq=mirror_phase(parent,gamma,end,ctx,rng,best_score,best_seq)
       cum += frac
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
       parent=normalize_sequence(best_seq)
       rem=max(0.0, deadline-time.monotonic())
       if rem > 5:
          t_peak=time.monotonic() + 0.12*rem
          t_peak=min(t_peak, deadline)
          parent,best_score,best_seq=peak_hill_climb(parent,t_peak,ctx,rng,best_score,best_seq)
          if time.monotonic() < deadline:
             rem2=max(0.0, deadline-time.monotonic())
             t_rand=time.monotonic()+0.5*rem2
             t_rand=min(t_rand, deadline)
             parent,best_score,best_seq=random_hill_climb(parent,t_rand,ctx,rng,best_score,best_seq)
    return parent,best_score,best_seq
```

`add_candidate` and `sort_pool`:
```
def add_candidate(pool, seq, score):
    if seq is None: return
    if not np.isfinite(score): score=np.inf
    seq=np.asarray(seq,dtype=float)
    seq=np.where(np.isfinite(seq),seq,0.0)
    seq=np.clip(seq,0,1000.0)
    pool.append([float(score), seq.copy()])
    if len(pool)>5:
       worst=0
       for i in range(1,len(pool)):
          if pool[i][0] > pool[worst][0]: worst=i
       pool.pop(worst)

def sort_pool(pool):
    # insertion sort by score
    for i in range(1,len(pool)):
       item=pool[i]
       j=i-1
       while j>=0 and pool[j][0] > item[0]:
          pool[j+1]=pool[j]
          j-=1
       pool[j+1]=item
```
If scores inf, okay.

def to_return_list(seq):
    out=[]
    if seq is None: return out
    for x in np.asarray(seq,dtype=float).reshape(-1):
       xf=float(x)
       if not np.isfinite(xf): xf=0.0
       if xf<0.0: xf=0.0
       if xf>1000.0: xf=1000.0
       out.append(xf)
    return out
```

Detailed helper definitions:

```
def next_power_of_two(x):
    x=int(x)
    if x <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (x-1).bit_length()
```

```
def make_context(n):
    n=int(n)
    lin_size=next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
    cross_size=next_power_of_two(3*n-2 if n>1 else 1)
    if n == 1:
       idx=np.arange(0,0,-1?) # start=0 stop=-1 => [0]
    else:
       idx=np.arange(2*n-2, n-2, -1, dtype=np.int64)
    return (lin_size, cross_size, idx)
```
For n=1, 2*n-2=0, n-2=-1 -> arange(0,-1,-1) returns [0]. Good.

`conv_score`:
```
def conv_score(a, ctx):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([]),0.0,np.inf
    b=linear_convolution(a,ctx)
    if b.size: mb=float(b.max())
    else: mb=0.0
    score=2.0*n*mb if np.isfinite(mb) else np.inf
    return b,mb,score
```

`soft_objective`:
```
def soft_objective(b, theta):
    if theta <=0 or b.size==0: return np.inf
    t=theta*b
    tmax=float(t.max())
    if not np.isfinite(tmax) or tmax <=0.0:
        return float(b.max()) if b.size else np.inf
    e=np.exp(t - tmax)
    se=float(e.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(se) or se <=0.0:
        return tmax/theta
    return (tmax + float(np.log(se))) / theta
```
If theta huge and tmax=0? b all zeros. Return 0? If max zero, score 0? impossible. tmax zero -> return 0.
Maybe if tmax very small, division okay. Use if tmax <= 0 return float(b.max()) (0). Good.

`score_only`:
```
def score_only(a, ctx):
    a=normalize_sequence(a)
    b,mb,s=conv_score(a,ctx)
    return a,s
```

`cross_correlate` as above. Use `ctx[1]`, `ctx[2]`.
Need ensure weights sum not zero. In dense FFT, if weights tiny, okay. In sparse, threshold relative to wmax; if wmax==0 return zeros.

```
def cross_correlate(a, weights, ctx):
    n=a.size
    L=2*n-1
    if weights.size != L:
       w=np.zeros(L,dtype=np.float64)
       m=min(weights.size,L)
       if m>0: w[:m]=weights[:m]
       weights=w
    wmax=float(weights.max()) if weights.size else 0.0
    if wmax > 0.0:
       # significant indices
       thr = max(1e-15, 1e-10 * wmax) # maybe too high for low gamma? For low gamma weights all positive ~1/L=3e-5, wmax maybe? wmax > threshold if not all equal. If weights nearly uniform, wmax~1/L, thr=1e-15, selects all -> dense. Good.
       idxs=np.nonzero(weights > thr)[0]
    else: idxs=np.empty(0,dtype=int)
    if idxs.size < 256:
       c=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
       for kk in idxs:
          k=int(kk)
          start=k-(n-1)
          if start<0: start=0
          stop=k+1
          if stop>n: stop=n
          if stop<=start: continue
          j_end=k-(stop-1)
          j_start=k-start
          if j_end <0: j_end=0
          if j_start >= n: j_start=n-1
          val=float(weights[kk])
          if val ==0.0: continue
          c[start:stop] += val * a[j_end:j_start+1][::-1]
       return c
    else:
       cross_size=ctx[1]; idx=ctx[2]
       w_rev=weights[::-1]
       fa=np.fft.fft(a, n=cross_size)
       fw=np.fft.fft(w_rev, n=cross_size)
       full=np.fft.ifft(fa*fw).real
       return full[idx]
```
If n large and dense, returns real maybe tiny negative? c from sum of positive weights*a can be slightly negative due FFT. We can `np.maximum(c, 0?)`? The theoretical c nonnegative (a,weights nonnegative). FFT numerical small negative ~1e-16. We can clip: `return np.maximum(full[idx], 0.0)`? But if c has small negative, d mean maybe okay. Clipping to 0 might bias but tiny. Use `c=full[idx]; c[c<0]=0.0` only for values > -1e-12? Simpler `return np.maximum(full[idx], 0.0)`. If some true c small but positive, no issue. However if numerical negative larger due length? Shouldn't. Use maximum.
Sparse c exact nonnegative.

`mirror_phase`:
```
def mirror_phase(a, gamma, phase_deadline, ctx, rng, best_score, best_seq):
    n=a.size
    parent=normalize_sequence(a)
    best_score_local=float(best_score) if best_score is not None else np.inf
    if best_seq is None:
       best_seq_local=parent.copy()
       if not np.isfinite(best_score_local): best_score_local, _ = score_only(parent, ctx)? 
    else:
       best_seq_local=np.array(best_seq, copy=True)
    no=0
    while time.monotonic() < phase_deadline:
       b, mb, score_p = conv_score(parent, ctx)
       if not np.isfinite(score_p): break
       if score_p < best_score_local:
          best_score_local=score_p; best_seq_local=parent.copy()
       if b.size ==0 or mb <=0: break
       theta = gamma / mb
       if not np.isfinite(theta) or theta <=0: break
       tshift = theta*(b-mb)
       e=np.exp(np.clip(tshift, -50, 50)) # max0, clip lower to avoid underflow? underflow okay, clip not needed but okay.
       ws=float(e.sum())
       if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <=0:
          sb=float(b.sum())
          if sb>0: w=b/sb
          else: break
       else:
          w=e/ws
       c=cross_correlate(parent,w,ctx)
       s=2.0*c
       mean_s=float(s.mean())
       d=s-mean_s
       std=float(d.std())
       if not np.isfinite(std) or std <= 1e-18:
          no += 5
       else:
          dd=d/std
          G_p=soft_objective(b, theta)
          accepted=False
          for eta in (0.2,0.08,0.03,0.012,0.005):
             arg=np.clip(-eta*dd, -8.0, 8.0)
             cand=normalize_sequence(parent*np.exp(arg))
             b_c, mb_c, score_c=conv_score(cand, ctx)
             if not np.isfinite(score_c): continue
             G_c=soft_objective(b_c, theta)
             accept=False
             if score_c + 1e-12 < score_p:
                accept=True
             elif np.isfinite(G_c) and np.isfinite(G_p) and G_c + 1e-12 < G_p:
                tol = 1e-6 + 5e-4*(no/10.0)
                if score_c <= score_p * (1.0 + tol):
                    accept=True
             if accept:
                parent=cand; score_p=score_c; no=0
                if score_p < best_score_local: update
                accepted=True
                break
          if not accepted:
             no +=1
             if no % 20 == 0:
                sigma=0.05 if no<60 else 0.12
                noise=rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n)
                cand=normalize_sequence(parent*np.exp(np.clip(noise,-5,5)))
                b_c,mb_c,score_c=conv_score(cand,ctx)
                if np.isfinite(score_c):
                   G_c=soft_objective(b_c,theta)  # but theta old; okay
                   if score_c < score_p or (np.isfinite(G_c) and G_c < G_p and score_c < score_p*1.01):
                      parent=cand; score_p=score_c; no=0
                      if score_p<best_score_local: update
             if no > 120:
                if best_seq_local is not None:
                   parent=normalize_sequence(best_seq_local)
                   no=0
                else:
                   break
    # final ensure best
    if best_seq_local is None:
       best_seq_local=parent.copy(); best_score_local=score_p? (may not defined if b) ; fallback score_only
    return parent,best_score_local,np.asarray(best_seq_local)
```
In random nudge, `G_p` defined before loop. If no>20 and G_p not defined? It is defined before candidates. Good.
But if std small, G_p not defined; random skip? We can move G_p computation before std? It needs b,mb,theta. Do after weights? Do `G_p=soft_objective(b, theta)` before std to use in random. Good.

def peak_hill_climb(a, phase_deadline, ctx, rng, best_score, best_seq):
   n=a.size; parent=normalize_sequence(a); best_score_local=...; best_seq_local=...
   no=0
   while time.monotonic() < phase_deadline:
      b,mb,score=conv_score(parent,ctx)
      if not finite: break
      if score<best: update
      topk=min(32,b.size)
      if topk<=0: break
      peaks=np.argsort(-b[:topk?])? argsort full length 16383, okay.
      s=np.zeros(n)
      for kk in peaks: ...
      std_s=s.std()? we need d.
      d=s-s.mean(); std=d.std()
      if std <= 1e-18: no+=1; continue
      med=float(np.median(parent))
      scale=med/(std+1e-300)
      accepted=False
      for f in (0.5,0.2,0.08,0.03,0.012):
         cand=parent - f*scale*d
         cand=np.maximum(cand,0.0)
         cand=normalize_sequence(cand)
         _,_,cscore=conv_score(cand,ctx)
         if cscore + 1e-12 < score:
             parent=cand; score=cscore; accepted=True; no=0; update best; break
      if not accepted:
         no +=1
         if no%10==0:
            # random small block
            width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//100), max(2,n//4)))
            start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
            cand=parent.copy(); cand[start:start+width]*=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.1)))
            cand=normalize_sequence(cand); _,_,cscore=conv_score(cand,ctx)
            if cscore < score: parent=cand; score=cscore; no=0; update
         if no>80: break
   return parent,best_score_local,best_seq_local
```

`random_hill_climb`:
```
def random_hill_climb(a, phase_deadline, ctx, rng, best_score, best_seq):
   n=a.size; parent=normalize_sequence(a); best_local...
   _,_,score=conv_score(parent,ctx)
   no=0; sigma=0.15
   while time.monotonic() < phase_deadline:
      attempts=0
      while attempts<25 and time<deadline:
         attempts+=1
         if rng.random()<0.7:
            cand=parent.copy()
            width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//300), max(2,n//6)))
            start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
            factor=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
            cand[start:start+width]*=factor
         else:
            noise=rng.normal(0.0,0.03,size=n)
            cand=parent*np.exp(np.clip(noise,-4,4))
         cand=normalize_sequence(cand)
         _,_,cscore=conv_score(cand,ctx)
         if not finite: continue
         if cscore < best_local: update
         if cscore + 1e-12 < score:
            parent=cand; score=cscore; no=0; break
      else:
         no+=1
         if no%5==0: sigma=min(1.5, sigma*1.15)
         if no>120: break
   return parent,best_local,best_seq_local
```
The inner while attempts<25, if break, no reset; else no increments. If break outer? okay. Could be inefficient if many attempts fail; but phase small.

```python
"""
Step-function sequence optimizer for minimizing
  2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/sum(a)^2.

Strategy:
- Use inverse-square-root style asymmetric initials and a small parameter
  sweep to find a good non-constant start.
- Run mirror-descent / softmax-subgradient iterations for a smooth
  log-sum-exp approximation of the maximum convolution coefficient.
- Polish with exact peak-gradient hill climbing and random local blocks.
- Keep the best sequence seen and return it.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def next_power_of_two(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def make_context(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        n = 1
    lin_size = next_power_of_two(2 * n - 1)
    cross_size = next_power_of_two(max(1, 3 * n - 2))
    idx = np.arange(2 * n - 2, n - 2, -1, dtype=np.int64)
    return (lin_size, cross_size, idx)


def normalize_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.reshape(-1)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(a.shape, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size)
    a = a / s
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
    s = float(a.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
        a = a / s
    return a


def linear_convolution(a, ctx):
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    lin_size = ctx[0]
    fa = np.fft.fft(a, n=lin_size)
    b = np.fft.ifft(fa * fa, n=lin_size).real
    L = 2 * n - 1
    b = b[:L]
    return np.maximum(b, 0.0)


def conv_score(a, ctx):
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64), 0.0, np.inf
    b = linear_convolution(a, ctx)
    if b.size == 0:
        return b, 0.0, np.inf
    mb = float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mb):
        return b, 0.0, np.inf
    score = 2.0 * n * mb
    return b, mb, score


def score_only(a, ctx):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    _, _, s = conv_score(a, ctx)
    return a, s


def soft_objective(b, theta):
    if theta <= 0.0 or b.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    t = theta * b
    tmax = float(t.max())
    if not np.isfinite(tmax):
        return np.inf
    if tmax <= 0.0:
        return 0.0
    e = np.exp(t - tmax)
    se = float(e.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
        return tmax / theta
    return (tmax + float(np.log(se))) / theta


def cross_correlate(a, weights, ctx):
    """
    Return c_i = sum_j a_j * weights[i+j].
    Used for gradient-like terms of convolution-square objectives.
    """
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if weights.size != L:
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(weights.size, L)
        if m > 0:
            w[:m] = weights[:m]
        weights = w

    wmax = float(weights.max()) if weights.size else 0.0
    if wmax > 0.0:
        thr = max(1e-15, 1e-10 * wmax)
        idxs = np.nonzero(weights > thr)[0]
    else:
        idxs = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)

    if idxs.size < 256:
        c = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for kk in idxs:
            k = int(kk)
            start = k - (n - 1)
            if start < 0:
                start = 0
            stop = k + 1
            if stop > n:
                stop = n
            if stop <= start:
                continue
            j_end = k - (stop - 1)
            j_start = k - start
            if j_end < 0:
                j_end = 0
            if j_start >= n:
                j_start = n - 1
            val = float(weights[kk])
            if val == 0.0:
                continue
            c[start:stop] += val * a[j_end:j_start + 1][::-1]
        return c

    cross_size = ctx[1]
    idx = ctx[2]
    w_rev = weights[::-1]
    fa = np.fft.fft(a, n=cross_size)
    fw = np.fft.fft(w_rev, n=cross_size)
    full = np.fft.ifft(fa * fw).real
    c = full[idx]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def mirror_phase(a, gamma, phase_deadline, ctx, rng, best_score, best_seq):
    n = a.size
    parent = normalize_sequence(a)

    best_score_local = float(best_score) if best_score is not None else np.inf
    if best_seq is None:
        _, best_score_local = score_only(parent, ctx)
        best_seq_local = parent.copy()
    else:
        best_seq_local = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < phase_deadline:
        b, mb, score_p = conv_score(parent, ctx)
        if not np.isfinite(score_p):
            break
        if score_p < best_score_local:
            best_score_local = score_p
            best_seq_local = parent.copy()
        if b.size == 0 or mb <= 0.0:
            break

        theta = gamma / mb
        if not np.isfinite(theta) or theta <= 0.0:
            break

        G_p = soft_objective(b, theta)

        tshift = theta * (b - mb)
        e = np.exp(tshift)
        ws = float(e.sum(dtype=np.float64))
        if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
            sb = float(b.sum(dtype=np.float64))
            if sb > 0.0:
                w = b / sb
            else:
                break
        else:
            w = e / ws

        c = cross_correlate(parent, w, ctx)
        s = 2.0 * c
        d = s - float(s.mean())
        std = float(d.std())

        if np.isfinite(std) and std > 1e-18:
            dd = d / std
            accepted = False
            for eta in (0.2, 0.08, 0.03, 0.012, 0.005):
                arg = np.clip(-eta * dd, -8.0, 8.0)
                cand = normalize_sequence(parent * np.exp(arg))
                b_c, _, score_c = conv_score(cand, ctx)
                if not np.isfinite(score_c):
                    continue

                G_c = soft_objective(b_c, theta)
                accept = False

                if score_c + 1e-12 < score_p:
                    accept = True
                elif np.isfinite(G_c) and np.isfinite(G_p) and G_c + 1e-12 < G_p:
                    tol = 1e-6 + 5e-4 * (no_improve / 10.0)
                    if score_c <= score_p * (1.0 + tol):
                        accept = True

                if accept:
                    parent = cand
                    score_p = score_c
                    no_improve = 0
                    if score_p < best_score_local:
                        best_score_local = score_p
                        best_seq_local = parent.copy()
                    accepted = True
                    break

            if not accepted:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve % 20 == 0:
                    sigma = 0.05 if no_improve < 60 else 0.12
                    noise = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n)
                    cand = normalize_sequence(parent * np.exp(np.clip(noise, -5.0, 5.0)))
                    b_c, _, score_c = conv_score(cand, ctx)
                    if np.isfinite(score_c):
                        G_c = soft_objective(b_c, theta)
                        if score_c < score_p or (np.isfinite(G_c) and G_c < G_p and score_c < score_p * 1.01):
                            parent = cand
                            score_p = score_c
                            no_improve = 0
                            if score_p < best_score_local:
                                best_score_local = score_p
                                best_seq_local = parent.copy()

                if no_improve > 120:
                    if best_seq_local is not None:
                        parent = normalize_sequence(best_seq_local)
                        no_improve = 0
                    else:
                        break
        else:
            no_improve += 5
            if no_improve > 120:
                if best_seq_local is not None:
                    parent = normalize_sequence(best_seq_local)
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    break

    if best_seq_local is None:
        best_seq_local = parent.copy()
        _, best_score_local = score_only(best_seq_local, ctx)

    return parent, best_score_local, best_seq_local


def peak_hill_climb(a, phase_deadline, ctx, rng, best_score, best_seq):
    n = a.size
    parent = normalize_sequence(a)
    best_score_local = float(best_score) if best_score is not None else np.inf
    if best_seq is None:
        _, best_score_local = score_only(parent, ctx)
        best_seq_local = parent.copy()
    else:
        best_seq_local = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    no_improve = 0
    while time.monotonic() < phase_deadline:
        b, _, score = conv_score(parent, ctx)
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            break
        if score < best_score_local:
            best_score_local = score
            best_seq_local = parent.copy()

        if b.size == 0:
            break
        topk = min(32, b.size)
        peaks = np.argsort(-b)[:topk]

        s = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for kk in peaks:
            k = int(kk)
            start = k - (n - 1)
            if start < 0:
                start = 0
            stop = k + 1
            if stop > n:
                stop = n
            if stop <= start:
                continue
            j_end = k - (stop - 1)
            j_start = k - start
            s[start:stop] += 2.0 * parent[j_end:j_start + 1][::-1]

        d = s - float(s.mean())
        std = float(d.std())
        if not np.isfinite(std) or std <= 1e-18:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 80:
                break
            continue

        med = float(np.median(parent))
        scale = med / (std + 1e-300)
        accepted = False

        for f in (0.5, 0.2, 0.08, 0.03, 0.012):
            cand = parent - f * scale * d
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
            cand = normalize_sequence(cand)
            _, _, cscore = conv_score(cand, ctx)
            if np.isfinite(cscore) and cscore + 1e-12 < score:
                parent = cand
                score = cscore
                accepted = True
                no_improve = 0
                if score < best_score_local:
                    best_score_local = score
                    best_seq_local = parent.copy()
                break

        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve % 10 == 0:
                width = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 100), max(2, n // 4)))
                start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
                cand = parent.copy()
                factor = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.1)))
                cand[start:start + width] *= factor
                cand = normalize_sequence(cand)
                _, _, cscore = conv_score(cand, ctx)
                if np.isfinite(cscore) and cscore + 1e-12 < score:
                    parent = cand
                    score = cscore
                    no_improve = 0
                    if score < best_score_local:
                        best_score_local = score
                        best_seq_local = parent.copy()

            if no_improve > 80:
                break

    return parent, best_score_local, best_seq_local


def random_hill_climb(a, phase_deadline, ctx, rng, best_score, best_seq):
    n = a.size
    parent = normalize_sequence(a)
    best_score_local = float(best_score) if best_score is not None else np.inf
    if best_seq is None:
        _, best_score_local = score_only(parent, ctx)
        best_seq_local = parent.copy()
    else:
        best_seq_local = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    _, _, score = conv_score(parent, ctx)
    no_improve = 0
    sigma = 0.15

    while time.monotonic() < phase_deadline:
        attempts = 0
        while attempts < 25 and time.monotonic() < phase_deadline:
            attempts += 1
            if rng.random() < 0.7:
                width = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 300), max(2, n // 6)))
                start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
                cand = parent.copy()
                factor = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
                cand[start:start + width] *= factor
            else:
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=n)
                cand = parent * np.exp(np.clip(noise, -4.0, 4.0))

            cand = normalize_sequence(cand)
            _, _, cscore = conv_score(cand, ctx)
            if not np.isfinite(cscore):
                continue
            if cscore < best_score_local:
                best_score_local = cscore
                best_seq_local = cand.copy()
            if cscore + 1e-12 < score:
                parent = cand
                score = cscore
                no_improve = 0
                break
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve % 5 == 0:
                sigma = min(1.5, sigma * 1.15)
            if no_improve > 120:
                break

    return parent, best_score_local, best_seq_local


def make_base_sequence(n, variant, rng=None):
    n = int(n)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 1:
        t = idx / float(n - 1)
    else:
        t = np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)

    base_inv = (idx + 0.5) ** (-0.5)

    if variant == "constant":
        arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    elif variant == "inv1":
        arr = (idx + 1.0) ** (-0.5)
    elif variant == "inv_ramp":
        arr = base_inv * (1.0 + 0.5 * t)
    elif variant == "inv_revramp":
        arr = base_inv * (1.0 + 0.5 * (1.0 - t))
    elif variant == "sin":
        arr = base_inv * (1.0 + 0.3 * np.sin(np.pi * t))
    elif variant == "random":
        if rng is None:
            rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
        arr = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, size=n))
    elif variant == "height":
        try:
            hs = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            hs = np.where(np.isfinite(hs), hs, 0.0)
            hs = np.maximum(hs, 0.0)
            if hs.size > 0 and hs.sum() > 0.0:
                x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, hs.size)
                x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
                h = np.interp(x_new, x_old, hs)
                h = normalize_sequence(h)
                arr = 0.4 * h + 0.6 * normalize_sequence(base_inv)
            else:
                arr = base_inv
        except Exception:
            arr = base_inv
    else:
        arr = base_inv

    return normalize_sequence(arr)


def quick_param_search(n, ctx, deadline, rng, best_score, best_seq):
    n = int(n)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 1:
        t = idx / float(n - 1)
    else:
        t = np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)

    best_seq_local = normalize_sequence(best_seq)
    best_score_local = float(best_score) if best_score is not None else np.inf

    powers = (0.30, 0.42, 0.50, 0.60)
    offs = (0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.0)

    for p in powers:
        for off in offs:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            base = (idx + off) ** (-p)
            arrs = []
            arrs.append(base)
            arrs.append(base * (1.0 + 0.35 * t))
            arrs.append(base * (1.0 - 0.25 * t))
            arrs.append(base * (1.0 + 0.30 * np.sin(np.pi * t)))
            arrs.append(base + 0.40 * base[::-1])

            for arr in arrs:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                    break
                a = normalize_sequence(arr)
                _, _, sc = conv_score(a, ctx)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score_local:
                    best_score_local = sc
                    best_seq_local = a.copy()

    return best_seq_local, best_score_local


def optimize_start(a, n, ctx, start_time, time_budget, hard_deadline, rng, best_score, best_seq):
    if time_budget <= 0.0:
        return normalize_sequence(a), best_score, best_seq

    start_time = float(start_time)
    total = min(float(time_budget), max(0.0, float(hard_deadline) - start_time))
    local_end = start_time + total
    parent = normalize_sequence(a)

    schedule = [
        (2.0, 0.08),
        (5.0, 0.12),
        (10.0, 0.22),
        (20.0, 0.22),
        (40.0, 0.16),
        (80.0, 0.12),
        (160.0, 0.08),
    ]

    cum = 0.0
    for gamma, frac in schedule:
        if time.monotonic() >= local_end:
            break
        end = start_time + (cum + frac) * total
        if end <= time.monotonic():
            end = min(local_end, time.monotonic() + max(2.0, 0.01 * total))
        parent, best_score, best_seq = mirror_phase(
            parent, gamma, end, ctx, rng, best_score, best_seq
        )
        cum += frac

    if time.monotonic() < local_end:
        parent = normalize_sequence(best_seq)
        rem = max(0.0, local_end - time.monotonic())
        if rem > 5.0:
            t_peak = time.monotonic() + 0.12 * rem
            t_peak = min(t_peak, local_end)
            parent, best_score, best_seq = peak_hill_climb(
                parent, t_peak, ctx, rng, best_score, best_seq
            )
            if time.monotonic() < local_end:
                rem2 = max(0.0, local_end - time.monotonic())
                t_rand = time.monotonic() + 0.5 * rem2
                t_rand = min(t_rand, local_end)
                parent, best_score, best_seq = random_hill_climb(
                    parent, t_rand, ctx, rng, best_score, best_seq
                )

    return parent, best_score, best_seq


def exact_score_numpy(seq):
    a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return np.inf
    b = np.convolve(a, a)
    mb = float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mb):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * mb / (s * s))


def add_candidate(pool, seq, score):
    if seq is None:
        return
    if score is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        score = np.inf
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    seq = np.where(np.isfinite(seq), seq, 0.0)
    seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    pool.append([float(score), seq])
    if len(pool) > 5:
        worst = 0
        for i in range(1, len(pool)):
            if pool[i][0] > pool[worst][0]:
                worst = i
        pool.pop(worst)


def sort_pool(pool):
    for i in range(1, len(pool)):
        item = pool[i]
        j = i - 1
        while j >= 0 and pool[j][0] > item[0]:
            pool[j + 1] = pool[j]
            j -= 1
        pool[j + 1] = item


def to_return_list(seq):
    out = []
    if seq is None:
        return out
    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    for x in arr:
        xf = float(x)
        if not np.isfinite(xf):
            xf = 0.0
        if xf < 0.0:
            xf = 0.0
        if xf > 1000.0:
            xf = 1000.0
        out.append(xf)
    return out


def propose_candidate(seed=12345, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 2400.0

    try:
        seed_int = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed_int = 12345

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed_int % (2 ** 32))
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    if budget > 300.0:
        margin = 60.0
    else:
        margin = 5.0
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, budget - margin)

    if budget < 30.0:
        n = 256
    elif budget < 120.0:
        n = 1024
    elif budget < 600.0:
        n = 4096
    else:
        n = 8192

    ctx = make_context(n)

    a0 = make_base_sequence(n, "inv05", rng)
    best_seq, best_score = score_only(a0, ctx)
    print(f"initial n={n} score={best_score:.8f}", flush=True)

    qdeadline = min(deadline, t0 + 15.0)
    if qdeadline > time.monotonic():
        best_seq, best_score = quick_param_search(
            n, ctx, qdeadline, rng, best_score, best_seq
        )
        best_seq = normalize_sequence(best_seq)
        print(f"after quick param score={best_score:.8f}", flush=True)

    pool = []
    add_candidate(pool, best_seq, best_score)

    now = time.monotonic()
    opt_budget = max(0.0, deadline - now - 5.0)

    if opt_budget > 10.0:
        primary_budget = 0.8 * opt_budget
        a1, best_score, best_seq = optimize_start(
            best_seq, n, ctx, now, primary_budget, deadline, rng, best_score, best_seq
        )
        add_candidate(pool, best_seq, best_score)
        print(f"after primary score={best_score:.8f}", flush=True)

        now = time.monotonic()
        target = 1.5027
        if best_score > target + 0.002 and now < deadline - 60.0:
            rem = deadline - now - 5.0
            if rem > 60.0:
                base2 = make_base_sequence(n, "inv_ramp", rng)
                blend2 = normalize_sequence(0.8 * base2 + 0.2 * normalize_sequence(best_seq))
                a2, best_score, best_seq = optimize_start(
                    blend2, n, ctx, now, 0.6 * rem, deadline, rng, best_score, best_seq
                )
                add_candidate(pool, best_seq, best_score)
                print(f"after secondary score={best_score:.8f}", flush=True)

        now = time.monotonic()
        if best_score > target + 0.01 and now < deadline - 40.0:
            rem = deadline - now - 5.0
            if rem > 40.0:
                base3 = make_base_sequence(n, "constant", rng)
                blend3 = normalize_sequence(0.5 * base3 + 0.5 * normalize_sequence(best_seq))
                a3, best_score, best_seq = optimize_start(
                    blend3, n, ctx, now, 0.5 * rem, deadline, rng, best_score, best_seq
                )
                add_candidate(pool, best_seq, best_score)
                print(f"after tertiary score={best_score:.8f}", flush=True)

    sort_pool(pool)
    if pool:
        final_seq = pool[0][1]
        final_approx = pool[0][0]
    else:
        final_seq = best_seq
        final_approx = best_score

    final_exact = np.inf
    hard_end = t0 + max(10.0, budget) - 5.0

    if pool and time.monotonic() + 25.0 < hard_end:
        eq = exact_score_numpy(pool[0][1])
        if np.isfinite(eq) and eq < final_exact:
            final_exact = eq
            final_seq = pool[0][1]

    if len(pool) > 1 and time.monotonic() + 55.0 < hard_end:
        eq = exact_score_numpy(pool[1][1])
        if np.isfinite(eq) and eq < final_exact:
            final_exact = eq
            final_seq = pool[1][1]

    if not np.isfinite(final_exact) and final_approx < final_exact:
        final_exact = final_approx

    ans = to_return_list(final_seq)
    if not ans:
        ans = to_return_list(make_base_sequence(1024, "inv05", rng))

    if np.isfinite(final_exact):
        print(f"final len={len(ans)} approx_or_exact={final_exact:.8f}", flush=True)
    else:
        print(f"final len={len(ans)} approx={final_approx:.8f}", flush=True)

    return ans


except Exception:
    # Robust fallback in case something unexpected happens during search.
    try:
        fallback = make_base_sequence(1024, "inv05", None)
        return to_return_list(fallback)
    except Exception:
        return [1.0] * 64
```
